namespace = guanzhi

# 册封唐爵. root = scope:actor (君主), scope:recipient = 受封者.
# 9750 爵级(写 var:guanzhi_tj_rank) -> 郡级: 州->郡; 县级: 州->郡->县.

guanzhi.9750 = {
	type = character_event
	title = guanzhi.9750.t
	desc = guanzhi.9750.desc
	theme = court
	left_portrait = { character = scope:recipient }
	right_portrait = { character = root }

	immediate = {
		debug_log = "GUANZHI 9750 fired"
	}

	option = {
		name = gztj_rank_guogong
		scope:recipient = {
			set_variable = { name = guanzhi_tj_rank value = 1 }
		}
		trigger_event = { id = guanzhi.9756 }
	}

	option = {
		name = gztj_rank_jungong
		scope:recipient = {
			set_variable = { name = guanzhi_tj_rank value = 2 }
		}
		trigger_event = { id = guanzhi.9760 }
	}

	option = {
		name = gztj_rank_xiangong
		scope:recipient = {
			set_variable = { name = guanzhi_tj_rank value = 3 }
		}
		trigger_event = { id = guanzhi.9780 }
	}

	option = {
		name = gztj_rank_xianhou
		scope:recipient = {
			set_variable = { name = guanzhi_tj_rank value = 4 }
		}
		trigger_event = { id = guanzhi.9780 }
	}

	option = {
		name = gztj_rank_xianbo
		scope:recipient = {
			set_variable = { name = guanzhi_tj_rank value = 5 }
		}
		trigger_event = { id = guanzhi.9780 }
	}

	option = {
		name = gztj_rank_xianzi
		scope:recipient = {
			set_variable = { name = guanzhi_tj_rank value = 6 }
		}
		trigger_event = { id = guanzhi.9780 }
	}

	option = {
		name = gztj_rank_xiannan
		scope:recipient = {
			set_variable = { name = guanzhi_tj_rank value = 7 }
		}
		trigger_event = { id = guanzhi.9780 }
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi.option.cancel
	}
}

guanzhi.6000 = {

	right_portrait = { character = root }

	immediate = {
		debug_log = "GUANZHI tj county menu 6000 opened"
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = var:guanzhi_tj_page } }
			set_variable = { name = guanzhi_tj_page value = 0 }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI tj page var was missing, set 0"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { var:guanzhi_tj_page = 0 }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI tj page var = 0 (page0 options visible)"

	title = gzj_jun_13
	desc = gztj_pick_xian_desc
	theme = court
	left_portrait = { character = scope:recipient }
	right_portrait = { character = root }

	immediate = {
		debug_log = "GUANZHI tj county menu 6013 opened"
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { exists = var:guanzhi_tj_page } }
			set_variable = { name = guanzhi_tj_page value = 0 }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI tj page var was missing, set 0"
		}
		if = {
			limit = { var:guanzhi_tj_page = 0 }
			debug_log = "GUANZHI tj page var = 0 (page0 options visible)"
		}
		else = {
			debug_log = "GUANZHI tj page var NOT 0!"
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = gztj_place_xian_0205
		trigger = { guanzhi_tj_page_trigger = { PAGE = 0 } }
		scope:recipient = { guanzhi_tangjue_grant_xian_0205 = yes }
	}

	option = {
		name = gztj_place_xian_0206
		trigger = { guanzhi_tj_page_trigger = { PAGE = 0 } }
		scope:recipient = { guanzhi_tangjue_grant_xian_0206 = yes }
	}

	option = {
		name = gztj_place_xian_0207
		trigger = { guanzhi_tj_page_trigger = { PAGE = 0 } }
		scope:recipient = { guanzhi_tangjue_grant_xian_0207 = yes }
	}

	option = {
		name = gztj_place_xian_0208
		trigger = { guanzhi_tj_page_trigger = { PAGE = 0 } }
		scope:recipient = { guanzhi_tangjue_grant_xian_0208 = yes }
	}

	option = {
		name = gztj_place_xian_0209
		trigger = { guanzhi_tj_page_trigger = { PAGE = 0 } }
		scope:recipient = { guanzhi_tangjue_grant_xian_0209 = yes }

	option = {

		trigger = { guanzhi_tj_page_trigger = { PAGE = 0 } }

	option = {

		scope:recipient = { guanzhi_tangjue_grant_xian_0211 = yes }
	}

	option = {
		name = gztj_place_xian_0212
		trigger = { guanzhi_tj_page_trigger = { PAGE = 0 } }
		scope:recipient = { guanzhi_tangjue_grant_xian_0212 = yes }
	}

	option = {
		name = gztj_place_xian_0213
		trigger = { guanzhi_tj_page_trigger = { PAGE = 0 } }

	option = {
		name = gztj_place_xian_0217

	option = {
		name = gztj_place_xian_0218
		trigger = { guanzhi_tj_page_trigger = { PAGE = 1 } }
		scope:recipient = { guanzhi_tangjue_grant_xian_0218 = yes }
	}

	option = {
		name = gztj_place_xian_0219
		trigger = { guanzhi_tj_page_trigger = { PAGE = 1 } }
		scope:recipient = { guanzhi_tangjue_grant_xian_0219 = yes }
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi.option.next
		if = {
			limit = { var:guanzhi_tj_page = 1 }
			set_variable = { name = guanzhi_tj_page value = 0 }
		}
		else = {
			change_variable = { name = guanzhi_tj_page add = 1 }
		}
		trigger_event = { id = guanzhi.6013 }
	}

	option = {
		name = guanzhi.option.back
		trigger_event = { id = guanzhi.9783 }
	}
}
